| 086 | Penalties and interest; LF131–131 | - **Late-filing surcharge (§ 152 AO).** Only for mandatory filers. The tax office may set it after a late return. | 132 | Example 2: married couple, classes III and V, one child, splitting; LF190–190 | Jonas earns EUR 72,000 (class III, 15 km on 200 days, so commuting is EUR 1,140 and the EUR 1,230 lump sum is higher). Mia earns EUR 18,000 (class V) and pays EUR 200 union dues; she has no other job costs. One child, Kindergeld EUR 3,108 for the year. Both in statutory health insurance at a 2.9% additional rate, both work outside Saxony (so care is 1.8% each), no church. Because one spouse was in class V, they must file (§ 46(2) no. 3a EStG). Wage tax withheld on both certificates together: EUR 9,414 (invented case; worked out with the § 39b(2) EStG formula for these facts: class III EUR 7,564, class V EUR 1,850 at the 14% minimum; a | a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF121 | \| Mandatory filers, own return \| By 31 July 2027 (seven months after year end, § 149(2) AO). 31 July 2027 is a Saturday, so the deadline moves to Monday 2 August 2027 (§ 108(3) AO) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF124, Deadlines and filing  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF122 | \| Mandatory filers, prepared by a tax adviser \| By the last day of February of the second following year (§ 149(3) AO): 29 February 2028. | a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF123 | \| Voluntary filers \| Four-year assessment period (§ 169(2) no. 2 AO), starting at the end of 2026 because there is no duty to file (§ 170(1) AO): the 2026 return must reach the tax office by 31 December 2030. The ELSTER 2025 guide states the same rule for 2025 (31 December 2029) and says it cannot be extended \| | Exact wording retained at current LF126, Deadlines and filing  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF124 | \| Payment \| An extra payment is due one month after the notice (§ 36(4) EStG). The tax office may also set quarterly prepayments for later years \| | Exact wording retained at current LF127, Deadlines and filing  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF128 | - **Late-filing surcharge (§ 152 AO).** Only for mandatory filers. The tax office may set it after a late return. It must set it if the return is not in within 14 months after year end (for 2026: by 29 February 2028) unless an extension was granted, the tax is nil, or the tax does not exceed the wage tax withheld and prepayments (§ 152(2) and (3) AO). Amount: 0.25% per month or part month of the tax less withheld tax and prepayments, at least EUR 25 per month, at most EUR 25,000 (§ 152(5) and (10) AO). An employee due a refund is not hit by the mandatory surcharge. | Exact wording retained at current LF131, Penalties and interest  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF129 | - **Interest on the balance (§ 233a AO).** Runs from 15 months after year end, so from 1 April 2028 for 2026, until the notice takes effect. 0.15% per full month, 1.8% a year (§ 238(1a) AO), on the balance rounded down to EUR 50. Refunds earn the same interest, at the earliest from the day the tax was paid. | Exact wording retained at current LF132, Penalties and interest  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF130 | - **Late payment (§ 240 AO).** 1% per started month on the overdue amount rounded down to EUR 50. | Exact wording retained at current LF133, Penalties and interest DE-R01: business-car commuting positive-difference and actual/logbook method supplied in current Cars/Commuting section; separate private-use method retained. |

Foreign tax credits under § 34c EStG are not covered here. | Exact wording retained at current LF137, Cross-border points  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF135 | - **Moving in or out in 2026.** Months of limited liability and German-exempt foreign income are handled under § 2(7) and § 32b EStG; the pack has no method yet. For shares and departure rules see leaving-germany-exit-tax. | Exact wording retained at current LF138, Cross-border points  |

| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF187 | Jonas earns EUR 72,000 (class III, 15 km on 200 days, so commuting is EUR 1,140 and the EUR 1,230 lump sum is higher). Mia earns EUR 18,000 (class V) and pays EUR 200 union dues; she has no other job costs. One child, Kindergeld EUR 3,108 for the year. Both in statutory health insurance at a 2.9% additional rate, both work outside Saxony (so care is 1.8% each), no church. Because one spouse was in class V, they must file (§ 46(2) no. 3a EStG). Wage tax withheld on both certificates together: EUR 9,414 (invented case; worked out with the § 39b(2) EStG formula for these facts: class III EUR 7,564, class V EUR 1,850 at the 14% minimum; a payroll run can differ by a few euros). | Exact wording retained at current LF190, Example 2: married couple, classes III and V, one child, splitting DE-R01: business-car commuting positive-difference and actual/logbook method supplied in current Cars/Commuting section; separate private-use method retained. |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF100 |    - Business travel, removal for work, second household, job application costs, account fees for salary. | Exact wording retained at current LF100, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF101 |    = **Income from employment.** | Exact wording retained at current LF101, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF102 | 3. **Add other income** (from the sibling skills) to get the sum of income (Summe der Einkünfte). Less the single parent relief where it applies (§ 24b(1) EStG deducts it from the sum of income) = **total income (Gesamtbetrag der Einkünfte)**. | Exact wording retained at current LF102, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF103 | 4. **Less special expenses (Sonderausgaben):** | Exact wording retained at current LF103, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF104 |    - Pension contributions: employee plus employer share to the statutory scheme, up to the cap, minus the tax-free employer share. In practice: the employee share. | Exact wording retained at current LF104, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF105 |    - Basic health and care insurance (employee share; statutory health reduced by 4% where sick pay is covered). Unemployment, disability and liability insurance count only while the EUR 1,900 cap is not already used by basic health and care (§ 10(4) sentence 4 EStG). For most employees it is. | Exact wording retained at current LF105, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF106 |    - Church tax paid, donations, first training up to EUR 6,000, childcare (§ 10(1) no. 5 EStG), or the EUR 36 lump sum. | Exact wording retained at current LF106, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF107 | 5. **Less extraordinary burdens** above the reasonable own share, maintenance payments, disability lump sums. | Exact wording retained at current LF107, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF108 |    = **Income (Einkommen).** | Exact wording retained at current LF108, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF109 | 6. **Child allowances or Kindergeld (§ 31 EStG).** The tax office compares. If the tax saving from the allowances is greater than the year's Kindergeld, it deducts the allowances and adds the Kindergeld to the tax. Otherwise Kindergeld stands and no allowance is deducted. The allowances are always used for the solidarity surcharge and church tax base (§ 3(2) SolzG, § 51a(2) EStG). | Exact wording retained at current LF109, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF110 |    = **Taxable income (zu versteuerndes Einkommen)**, rounded down to euros. | Exact wording retained at current LF110, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF111 | 7. **Tariff tax** (basic or splitting). Wage replacement benefits and treaty-exempt foreign pay raise the rate but are not taxed themselves (§ 32b EStG). | Exact wording retained at current LF111, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF112 | 8. **Less tax credits:** § 35a household services and craftsmen. | Exact wording retained at current LF112, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF113 | 9. **Plus solidarity surcharge and church tax.** | Exact wording retained at current LF113, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF114 | 10. **Less wage tax, surcharge and church tax withheld** (certificate lines 4 to 7). The difference is the refund or extra payment. | Exact wording retained at current LF114, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF116 | **Losses.** A loss from another income type (for example rental) reduces employment income in the same year. A remaining loss can be carried back one year or forward without time limit under § 10d EStG; that mechanism is not set out here. | Source-corrected loss carryback topic at current LF116: actual10d(1) includes immediately preceding and second preceding year; old one-year assertion is not retained as correct.  |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF122 | \| Form \| Main form (Hauptvordruck) with Anlage N per employed spouse, Anlage Vorsorgeaufwand, Anlage Kind per child, Anlage Haushaltsnahe Aufwendungen, Anlage Sonderausgaben, Anlage Außergewöhnliche Belastungen, Anlage N-AUS for foreign pay. Form names from the ELSTER 2025 guide; the 2026 forms are not yet published \| | Exact wording retained at current LF122, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF123 | \| Channel \| ELSTER (Mein ELSTER or third-party software). Electronic filing is compulsory only where there is farming, trade or self-employed income and the case is not one of § 46(2) no. 2 to 8 EStG; otherwise paper is allowed (§ 25(4) EStG) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF123, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF124 | \| Mandatory filers, own return \| By 31 July 2027 (seven months after year end, § 149(2) AO). 31 July 2027 is a Saturday, so the deadline moves to Monday 2 August 2027 (§ 108(3) AO) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF124, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF125 | \| Mandatory filers, prepared by a tax adviser \| By the last day of February of the second following year (§ 149(3) AO): 29 February 2028. Unlike 2020 to 2024, no special extension applies to 2026 (Art. 97 § 36 EGAO covers only up to 2024). The tax office can call for an earlier return (§ 149(4) AO) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF125, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF126 | \| Voluntary filers \| Four-year assessment period (§ 169(2) no. 2 AO), starting at the end of 2026 because there is no duty to file (§ 170(1) AO): the 2026 return must reach the tax office by 31 December 2030. The ELSTER 2025 guide states the same rule for 2025 (31 December 2029) and says it cannot be extended \| | Exact wording retained at current LF126, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF127 | \| Payment \| An extra payment is due one month after the notice (§ 36(4) EStG). The tax office may also set quarterly prepayments for later years \| | Exact wording retained at current LF127, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF131 | - **Late-filing surcharge (§ 152 AO).** Only for mandatory filers. The tax office may set it after a late return. It must set it if the return is not in within 14 months after year end (for 2026: by 29 February 2028) unless an extension was granted, the tax is nil, or the tax does not exceed the wage tax withheld and prepayments (§ 152(2) and (3) AO). Amount: 0.25% per month or part month of the tax less withheld tax and prepayments, at least EUR 25 per month, at most EUR 25,000 (§ 152(5) and (10) AO). An employee due a refund is not hit by the mandatory surcharge. | Exact wording retained at current LF131, Penalties and interest  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF132 | - **Interest on the balance (§ 233a AO).** Runs from 15 months after year end, so from 1 April 2028 for 2026, until the notice takes effect. 0.15% per full month, 1.8% a year (§ 238(1a) AO), on the balance rounded down to EUR 50. Refunds earn the same interest, at the earliest from the day the tax was paid. | Exact wording retained at current LF132, Penalties and interest  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF133 | - **Late payment (§ 240 AO).** 1% per started month on the overdue amount rounded down to EUR 50. | Exact wording retained at current LF133, Penalties and interest DE-R01: business-car commuting positive-difference and actual/logbook method supplied in current Cars/Commuting section; separate private-use method retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF137 | - **Foreign pay of a resident.** Pay exempt in Germany under a tax treaty is still declared (Anlage N-AUS) and raises the rate on the rest (§ 32b EStG). Foreign tax credits under § 34c EStG are not covered here. | Exact wording retained at current LF137, Cross-border points  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF138 | - **Moving in or out in 2026.** Months of limited liability and German-exempt foreign income are handled under § 2(7) and § 32b EStG; the pack has no method yet. For shares and departure rules see leaving-germany-exit-tax. | Exact wording retained at current LF138, Cross-border points  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF139 | - **Spouse living abroad.** An EU/EEA national may get joint assessment with a spouse resident in the EU/EEA under § 1a EStG; filing is then mandatory (§ 46(2) no. 7 EStG). Not covered further. | Exact wording retained at current LF139, Cross-border points  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF140 | - **Non-residents with German pay** (§ 1(3) election, § 46(2) no. 9 EStG): use de-non-resident-tax. Swiss cross-border commuters: refuse here.
